# VII. Work packages

## WP0 — immutable baseline and ownership reconciliation

Estimate: **0.45–0.65 week**. No dependencies.

- Verify custody source `7ac50c12e8e4d90988fee603ffdb9896e5628792`.
- Capture immutable counts and inventories: 19 capability manifests, four schema manifests, `_core`, 64 executors, 12 orchestrators, 10 elements, eight rendering extensions, four database packs, and 18 existing skills.
- Record the 23 current product directories, deletion of `builtin`, and final 22-pack expectation.
- Capture every active customization surface and initial owner classification.
- Record the 49 `builtin.*` aliases and all other deprecated aliases as intended deletions.
- Capture current database ordering, migration identities/checksums, application/open behavior, repositories, vocabulary, CLI/SDK/bridge behavior, doctor, backup, restore, and installed-wheel behavior.
- Establish the four-pack behavior matrix and 15-row completion matrix.
- Record exact exclusions for `.oracle`, planning artifacts, tests, golden authoring inputs, build outputs, caches, unrelated training manifests, and non-pack component formats.
- Do not build a wheel or run the full suite.
- Treat the runaway demo input as a temporary deterministic fixture.

Exit: every baseline surface is classified as pack, kernel, or intended deletion; no research question remains.

## WP1 — executable v2 contract, immutable model, resolver, snapshot, and audit tooling

Estimate: **1.3–1.75 weeks**. Depends on WP0.

Contract-freeze subgate first:

- Land the exact v2 JSON Schema and nested schemas above.
- Land immutable normalized types.
- Land the three golden fixtures and all invalid fixture classes.
- Land explicit provenance/resource types.
- Pass schema and normalization tests.
- Obtain cumulative review before any bundled manifest conversion.

Then:

- Evolve `PackDefinition` rather than add a parallel product model.
- Implement the only v2 `pack.yaml` loader.
- Delete fallback parsing from the new path.
- Implement confined resource handles and digest-verifying reads.
- Implement process-cached `BundledCatalog`.
- Implement dynamic candidate capture and `CatalogSnapshot`.
- Implement exact precedence, duplicate diagnostics, trust, and external-database rejection.
- Change local pack creation to emit strict capability-only v2.
- Implement the bounded resource classifier and targeted AST audit.
- Implement `scripts/canonical_pack_coverage.py` and its schemas.
- Adapt typed registry constructors to accept snapshot projections without changing service construction ownership.

Exit: schema, loader, normalized definition, provenance, resource resolver, snapshot semantics, resource audit, and coverage tooling are executable and fixture-proven.

## WP2 — bundled conversion, resources, ownership, and documentation

Estimate: **1.0–1.35 weeks**. Depends on WP1.

- Make references the combined database/repository/SDK/CLI/documentation exemplar without changing its three tables.
- Delete `builtin` and every deprecated alias.
- Add the five missing direct skills and repair media.
- Classify every bundled non-Python file using typed reachability, supplemental resource, or reasoned authoring exclusion.
- Explicitly declare requirements files, Blender’s service unit, templates, schemas, nested skills, fonts, SQL, and runtime assets.
- Move any pack-owned cross-root asset into its owner; classify genuinely shared kernel assets in the ledger.
- Switch bundled discovery to the v2 catalog.
- Regenerate capability/census output in checkable form.
- Run focused package-local ledger/resource/matrix checks without retaining extra immutable artifacts.

### Boundary A — canonical declaration foundation

After WP2:

- Generate immutable inventory/check/diff artifacts.
- Prove exactly 22 retained bundled product packs load.
- Prove no bundled `schema-pack.yaml` remains.
- Prove every bundled file is classified.

- Resolve findings and obtain a fresh pass before WP3.

- Delete schema-pack identity from the moved types and diagnostics.
- Add the synthetic core node and catalog-derived bundled database nodes.

## WP4 — operational, SDK, inspection, doctor, and agent convergence

Estimate: **1.1–1.5 weeks**. Depends on Boundary B.

Thread one snapshot or projection through:

- standard application startup;

- `DatabaseWriter` and the exclusive owner lock;
- static repository/service/CLI/bridge factories;
- backup/restore payload semantics;
- the eight-family public gateway and two nested mounts.

Raw database reads must use the shared non-mutating probe or an already-probed connection.

`python3 -m astrid.core.pack.cli inspect <id> [--json]` exposes:

- normalized identity;
- loader-derived provenance;
- contribution/capability categories;
- database dependencies, migrations, owned tables, and derived head;
- documentation;
- complete resource closure;
- rejected duplicate/stale candidate diagnostics where relevant.

Stable JSON uses a versioned envelope and sorted arrays. Text renders from that DTO.

Doctor adds:

- canonical bundled pack census;
- catalog/resource/documentation health;
- expected/applied/pending migration status by database owner;
- no mutations.

Generate/check `_core` census from the same snapshot.

Exit: every operational consumer agrees on one snapshot and composition; no consumer reparses a pack or rescans during its operation.

Estimate: **0.85–1.2 weeks**. Depends on WP4.

- Any fixed database tuple or name-based selection.
- Intermediate audit support that interprets legacy formats.

Keep exact gates:

- Prohibited active path checks for `schema-pack.yaml`, `pack.yml`, and `pack.json`.
- Exact import/AST checks for retired schema-pack modules, fixed builders/tuples, raw identity reconstruction, and independent rescans.
- Strict loader/catalog tests.
- Coverage and four-pack matrix checks.
- Resource classification and targeted AST checks.
- Generated census drift.
- Source/wheel semantic closure.

Packaging:

- Extend existing package-data patterns for every declared resource kind.
- Include all 22 `pack.yaml` files, SQL, structured skills/docs, requirements, service unit, schemas, templates, fonts, and current runtime assets.
- Exclude every `authoring_only` path.
- Add no packaging backend.
- Convert every wheel-building test/gate to either:

  - a source contract/inventory test;
  - a mocked builder-unit test; or
  - a consumer of `ASTRID_PREBUILT_WHEEL`.

- Add a prebuilt-wheel harness entry point, e.g. `InstalledArtifactHarness.from_wheel(path, ...)`.
- Update every current `build_once` test call site and final gate to consume the prebuilt wheel during authoritative validation.
- Retain `build_once` only as a non-authoritative developer convenience if still useful; no final or full-suite path may invoke it when `ASTRID_PREBUILT_WHEEL` is set.

### Boundary C — operational hard cut and packaged source

- Generate immutable inventory/check/diff artifacts.
- Run source package-data inventory checks, not a wheel build.

## WP6 — one-build authoritative validation and evidence closure

Estimate: **0.5–0.75 week**. Depends on Boundary C.

One authoritative owner performs one build:

```bash
python3 -m build --outdir .oracle/evidence/final-build
```

Require exactly one wheel in that directory, calculate its SHA-256, and set:

```bash
ASTRID_PREBUILT_WHEEL=<absolute path to that wheel>
```

No subsequent command may invoke `python -m build`, `build_once`, `pip wheel`, or another source build.

Using that artifact, run the required focused suite:

```bash
python3 -m pytest tests/packs tests/v10/test_catalog_migrations.py \
  tests/v10/test_m8_packaging.py tests/v10/test_pack_factoring.py \
  tests/v10/test_reference_repository.py tests/sdk/test_references.py \
  tests/sdk/test_extended_composition.py
```

Run doctor:

```bash
python3 -m astrid doctor
```

Run the source-isolated installed-wheel lanes through the prebuilt artifact:

- installed bundled catalog load;
- source/wheel definition comparison;
- bidirectional resource audit;
- documentation and `_core` census audit;
- fresh, upgraded, read-only-pending, and existing SQLite scenarios;
- external capability success/database rejection;
- references and runaway round trips;
- backup/restore;
- inspect text/JSON;
- doctor/inspect non-mutation;
- exact legacy gates;
- zero-unclassified coverage check.

Then run the full authoritative suite once, still with the same prebuilt wheel:

```bash
python3 -m pytest
```

If a suspected unrelated failure appears, reproduce only that failing scenario against the pinned source; do not run a second baseline suite or build.

Final artifacts include:

- One wheel and its SHA-256.
- Installed-lane records carrying that same hash.
- Final coverage inventory/check/diff.
- Final resource closure comparison.
- Final four-pack matrix.
- Final 15-row evidence matrix.
- Full/focused test receipts.
- Doctor and inspect receipts.
- Independent reviewer dispositions.

After all evidence passes:

- Obtain final Sol oracle review.
- Commit only reviewed paths.
- Push only `HEAD:refs/heads/megado/canonical-pack-beta`.
- Open the completed worktree.
- Do not merge, rebase, deploy, promote, or publish.

# VIII. Cumulative review cadence

1. **Contract-freeze subgate:** during WP1, before bundled conversion.
2. **Boundary A:** after WP2—v2 catalog, all bundled declarations, docs, and resource closure.
3. **Boundary B:** after WP3—catalog-derived database composition and migration hard cut.
4. **Boundary C:** after WP5—all consumers, deletions, audits, and source packaging configuration.
5. **Final:** after WP6—single-wheel and complete evidence closure.

At A, B, C, and final:

- Generate one immutable coverage inventory/check/diff artifact set.
- Regenerate relevant matrices.
- Converge code, receipts, artifacts, and status on one checkpoint.
- Run one cumulative independent review pass by default.
- Resolve accepted findings and obtain a fresh pass before continuing.

Package-local checks may run during implementation, but immutable cumulative artifacts are produced only at A, B, C, and final.

# X. Explicit anti-pattern rejections

- No hidden schema-pack subsystem. Only reusable migration/collision algorithms survive.
- No fixed composition disguise. Named packs appear only in expected fixtures and preservation evidence.
- No universal service locator. The catalog contains declarations; typed registries and static factories construct runtime objects.
- No runtime coverage ledger. It is audit-only.
- No YAML DDL or applied state.
- No manifest-supplied trust.
- No operation-internal rescanning.
- No lower-precedence partial contribution after duplicate-ID loss.
- No perfect/general static analysis. Resource proof is a bounded closed-world classification plus targeted known-API AST checks.
- No arbitrary authoring exclusion for known runtime assets.
- No dependency solver, release-version solving, project lock, enable/disable/purge, database uninstall, marketplace, signing, sandboxing, or UI.
- No external SQL.
- No dynamically unloadable kernel.
- No v1, alternate filenames, schema-less manifests, dual reads, compatibility exports, deprecated aliases, or fallbacks.
- No generalized repository/service/CLI/bridge factory framework.
- No custom packaging backend.
- No broad repository-wide regex policy scanner.
- No repeated wheel builds.
- No repeated immutable ledger/matrix artifacts between cumulative boundaries.
- No scope expansion into models, LoRAs, taxonomies, or unrelated kernel primitives.
- No success while an active bypass, unclassified surface, missing document, undeclared resource, source/wheel mismatch, or independent consumer rescan remains.

## Final disposition

This replacement closes every Wave 2 reopening with an executable boundary:

- v2 has an exact field grammar and normalization contract;
- one immutable `CatalogSnapshot` governs each operation;
- precedence, duplicates, trust, staleness, and external database rejection are deterministic;
- bundled resources have a bounded complete classification algorithm;
- the coverage ledger has concrete commands, schema, inputs, and diff semantics;
- migration dependencies compare exact integer migration heads with a defined graph and tie-break;
- installed proof uses one wheel built once;
- cumulative artifact ceremony is limited to A, B, C, and final.

The next Phase 3 action is an exact Sol `STABLE` check against this complete snapshot. If the response is exactly `STABLE`, run a fresh full settled-plan wave before accepting the plan as settled.
